def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Enhanced Quantum Kaleidoscope System")
    parser.add_argument("--data-dir", type=str, default="./data", help="Data directory")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8000, help="Port for web server")
    parser.add_argument("--auto-gen", action="store_true", help="Enable auto-generation mode")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # Create main components
    simulator = QuantumFieldSimulator(data_dir=args.data_dir)
    visualizer = VisualizationEngine(simulator)
    web_server = WebServer(
        simulator=simulator,
        visualizer=visualizer,
        port=args.port
    )

    if args.auto_gen:
        # Run in auto-generation mode
        asyncio.run(auto_generation_loop(simulator))  # Use asyncio.run to start the async loop
    else:
        # Start the web server (which also starts the simulation)
        web_server.start()

# File: quantum_kaleidoscope_integrated/quantum_kaleidoscope_launcher.py
def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Quantum Kaleidoscope Launcher")
    parser.add_argument("--data-dir", type=str, default="./data", help="Data directory")
    parser.add_argument("--main-port", type=int, default=8000, help="Port for main system")
    parser.add_argument("--visualizer-port", type=int, default=8081, help="Port for visualizer")
    parser.add_argument("--auto-gen", action="store_true", help="Enable auto-generation")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    process_mgr = launch_system(
        data_dir=args.data_dir,
        main_port=args.main_port,
        visualizer_port=args.visualizer_port,
        auto_gen=args.auto_gen
    )
    
    if not process_mgr:
        logger.error("Failed to launch system")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    try:
        while True:
            time.sleep(1)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        logger.info("Shutting down Quantum Kaleidoscope System...")
        process_mgr.stop_all()
        logger.info("System shutdown complete")
        sys.exit(0)

# File: quantum_kaleidoscope_integrated/Rebuilt_AI_System_with_GPT/run.py
def main(args):
    # Initialize core components
    node_manager = NodeManager()
    perspective_manager = PerspectiveManager()
    ai_core = AI_Core(node_manager, perspective_manager)
    gpt_processor = GPTProcessor(model_name=args.model_name, device=args.device)  # Initialize GPTProcessor

    try:
        # Load initial nodes and perspectives (if available)
        if args.load:
            node_manager.load_nodes()
            perspective_manager.load_perspectives()

        # Main program loop
        while True:
            print("\nEnter your query (or type 'exit' to quit, 'save' to save state, 'nodes' to list nodes, 'perspectives' to list perspectives):")
            user_input = input("> ")

            if user_input.lower() == 'exit':
                break
            elif user_input.lower() == 'save':
                node_manager.save_nodes()
                perspective_manager.save_perspectives()
                print("System state saved.")
            elif user_input.lower() == 'nodes':
                print(node_manager.get_all_nodes())
            elif user_input.lower() == 'perspectives':
                print(perspective_manager.get_all_perspectives())
            elif user_input.startswith("create_node"):
                try:
                    node_type = user_input.split(" ")[1]
                    node_manager.create_node(node_type)
                    print(f"Node of type {node_type} created.")
                except IndexError:
                    print("Usage: create_node <node_type>")
            elif user_input.startswith("connect_nodes"):
                try:
                    node_ids = user_input.split(" ")[1:]
                    if len(node_ids) == 2:
                        node_manager.connect_nodes(node_ids[0], node_ids[1])
                        print(f"Nodes {node_ids[0]} and {node_ids[1]} connected.")
                    else:
                        print("Usage: connect_nodes <node_id1> <node_id2>")
                except IndexError:
                    print("Usage: connect_nodes <node_id1> <node_id2>")

            else:
                # Process user input using the AI Core and GPT Processor
                ai_core.process_input(user_input, gpt_processor)  # Pass the GPTProcessor instance

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nShutting down...")
    finally:
        # Ensure resources are cleaned up
        node_manager.save_nodes()
        perspective_manager.save_perspectives()
        print("System state saved on exit.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run the AI Core with GPT.")
    parser.add_argument("--load", action="store_true", help="Load existing nodes and perspectives.")
    parser.add_argument("--model_name", type=str, default="EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B", help="Name of the GPT model to use.")
    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu", help="Device to run the model on (cuda or cpu).")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    main(args)

Okay, let's break down how to get this system running, what it does, and the role of a centralized task manager.

How to Get the System Running

Based on the provided code, particularly the quantum_kaleidoscope_launcher.py, here's the general process:

Set Up Dependencies:
The enhanced-quantum-kaleidoscope.py script includes a function setup_virtual_environment() that creates a virtual environment and installs necessary Python packages (numpy, websockets, flask, psutil, networkx). You might need to run this setup first.
The Rebuilt_AI_System_with_GPT also has a requirements.txt file, indicating dependencies for the AI part of the system (likely transformers and torch). You'll need to install these, probably using pip install -r requirements.txt.
Launch the System:
The quantum_kaleidoscope_launcher.py script is the main entry point. It uses argparse to handle command-line arguments:
--data-dir: Directory to store data (./data by default).
--main-port: Port for the main system's web server (default 8000).
--visualizer-port: Port for the visualizer's web server (default 8081).
--auto-gen: A flag to enable auto-generation mode (if the system has that capability).
Run the quantum_kaleidoscope_launcher.py script from the command line, providing any necessary arguments. This script will:
Launch the main system (enhanced-quantum-kaleidoscope.py).
Launch the visualizer (quantum_visualizer_new.py).
Access the System:
Once the scripts are running, you should be able to access the web interfaces:
Main System: http://localhost:8000 (or the port you specified)
Visualizer: http://localhost:8081 (or the port you specified)
The quantum_visualizer_new.py script indicates that it fetches data from the main system's API (/data).
What the System Does (Based on the Code)

This appears to be a complex system that combines:

Quantum-Inspired Neural Network Simulation:
enhanced-quantum-kaleidoscope.py contains the QuantumFieldSimulator, which simulates "quantum states" with properties like energy, coherence, and entanglement. It models their interactions and evolution.
The system processes text input, converts it into vector representations, creates quantum states, and generates "insights."
3D Visualization:
quantum_visualizer_new.py and the JavaScript (quantum-kaleidoscope-frontend.js) provide 3D visualization of the simulated quantum field. Nodes represent quantum states, and connections represent entanglement.
The visualization displays properties like energy, coherence, and connections.
AI Processing:
The Rebuilt_AI_System_with_GPT directory suggests the integration of a language model (GPT). It includes components for:
Managing nodes and perspectives in a network.
Processing user input with the GPT model.
Analyzing text structure and extracting entities.
Web Interface:
Flask web servers (enhanced-quantum-kaleidoscope.py and quantum_visualizer_new.py) provide access to the system through web pages.
Can a Centralized Task Manager Fix Problems and Get It Running?

A centralized task manager can definitely help, but it's not a magic bullet. Here's how it could address potential issues:

Problem: Complex Startup
Solution: A task manager (like the ProcessManager in quantum_kaleidoscope_launcher.py, but potentially more robust) can handle the coordinated startup of multiple processes (main system, visualizer, AI components), ensuring they start in the correct order and with the right dependencies.
Problem: Resource Management
Solution: A task manager can monitor CPU, memory, and network usage of each component, preventing any single process from hogging resources and potentially restarting processes if they crash or become unresponsive.
Problem: Inter-Process Communication
Solution: If components need to communicate in more complex ways than basic API calls, a task manager can facilitate this (e.g., using message queues or shared memory).
Problem: Error Handling and Logging
Solution: A centralized manager can aggregate logs from all components, making it easier to diagnose problems. It can also implement sophisticated error handling and recovery strategies.
Problem: Synchronization
Solution: For the "quantum" aspects, if precise timing or synchronization between components is critical, a task manager can help orchestrate this (though this is a very advanced use case).
Important Notes:

The existing quantum_kaleidoscope_launcher.py already implements a basic task manager (ProcessManager) for starting and stopping the main system and visualizer.
A more sophisticated task manager might use tools like Celery, Kubernetes, or even a custom solution built with threading/asyncio.
The key is that the task manager provides a layer of orchestration and monitoring above the individual components.
In summary, getting this system running involves careful setup of dependencies and launching the various Python scripts in the correct order. The system itself is a fascinating combination of quantum-inspired simulation, visualization, and AI. A centralized task manager is a valuable tool for ensuring the system runs smoothly, efficiently, and reliably.

class DataPipeline:  # Placeholder
    def __init__(self, max_queue_size: int, concurrency_enabled: bool):
        self.max_queue_size = max_queue_size
        self.concurrency_enabled = concurrency_enabled
        self.queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=max_queue_size)
        self.consumers = {}

    async def enqueue(self, data: Any):
        await self.queue.put(data)

    def register_consumer(self, consumer_id: str, consumer_func: Callable[[Any], Awaitable[Any]]):
        self.consumers[consumer_id] = consumer_func

    async def run(self):
        while True:
            data = await self.queue.get()
            for consumer_id, consumer_func in self.consumers.items():
                await consumer_func(data)
            self.queue.task_done()

Step 2: Define Missing Core Classes

Based on the previous analysis, we need to implement the following core classes to provide the foundation for the system's functionality:

Here's a possible implementation of these classes:

Python

# core/core_classes.py
import logging
import time
import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Callable
import networkx as nx
import asyncio

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class CoreLaws:
    """
    Defines fundamental laws governing node behavior and interactions.
    """

    def __init__(self, energy_decay_rate: float = 0.01, connection_strength_decay: float = 0.005):
        """
        Args:
            energy_decay_rate: Rate at which node energy decreases over time.
            connection_strength_decay: Rate at which connection strength decays.
        """
        self.energy_decay_rate = energy_decay_rate
        self.connection_strength_decay = connection_strength_decay

    def apply_energy_decay(self, node: 'CapabilityNode'):
        """Applies energy decay to a node."""
        node.energy = max(0.0, node.energy - self.energy_decay_rate)

    def update_connection_strength(self, connection: Dict[str, Any]):
        """Updates the strength of a connection."""
        connection["strength"] = max(0.0, connection["strength"] - self.connection_strength_decay)

class CapabilityNode:
    """
    Base class for nodes with specific processing capabilities.
    """

    def __init__(self, node_id: str, capabilities: List[str], core_laws: CoreLaws):
        """
        Args:
            node_id: Unique identifier for the node.
            capabilities: List of strings representing the node's processing capabilities.
            core_laws: CoreLaws instance governing node behavior.
        """
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.capabilities = capabilities
        self.core_laws = core_laws
        self.energy = 1.0
        self.connections: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}  # {target_id: {relationship: str, strength: float}}
        self.data_queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()  # Queue for incoming data
        self.last_processed: Optional[float] = None
        self.state: Dict[str, Any] = {}  # Dynamic state information
        self.processing_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None

    async def process_data(self, data: Any) -> Any:
        """
        Processes incoming data using the node's capabilities.
        This is the main method called by the DataPipeline.
        """
        await self.data_queue.put(data)  # Enqueue data
        if self.processing_task is None or self.processing_task.done():
            self.processing_task = asyncio.create_task(self._process_queue())  # Start processing
        return None  # Placeholder

    async def _process_queue(self):
        """
        Internal method to process data from the queue.
        """
        while not self.data_queue.empty():
            data = await self.data_queue.get()
            try:
                result = self.execute_capability(data)
                self.last_processed = time.time()
                self.core_laws.apply_energy_decay(self)  # Apply energy decay
                self._update_connections()  # Update connection strengths
                # ... (Handle result and send to other nodes/modules)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error processing data in node {self.node_id}: {e}", exc_info=True)
            finally:
                self.data_queue.task_done()

    @abstractmethod
    def execute_capability(self, data: Any) -> Any:
        """
        Executes a specific processing capability on the input data.
        To be implemented by subclasses (e.g., TextNode, VisualNode).
        """
        pass

    def add_connection(self, target_id: str, relationship: str, strength: float = 1.0):
        """Adds a connection to another node."""
        if target_id not in self.connections:
            self.connections[target_id] = {"relationship": relationship, "strength": strength}

    def get_connections(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Gets all connections of the node."""
        return self.connections

    def update_connection_strength(self, target_id: str):
        """Updates the strength of a connection."""
        if target_id in self.connections:
            self.core_laws.update_connection_strength(self.connections[target_id])

class NodeManager:
    """
    Manages the creation, retrieval, and connections of nodes.
    """

    def __init__(self, core_laws: CoreLaws):
        """
        Args:
            core_laws: CoreLaws instance for node governance.
        """
        self.nodes: Dict[str, CapabilityNode] = {}
        self.core_laws = core_laws

    def create_node(self, node_type: str, capabilities: List[str], initial_data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> CapabilityNode:
        """
        Creates a new node.
        """
        node_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        node = CapabilityNode(node_id=node_id, capabilities=capabilities, core_laws=self.core_laws)
        # ... (Initialize node properties based on initial_data)
        self.nodes[node_id] = node
        logger.info(f"Node created: {node_id} ({node_type})")
        return node

    def get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Optional[CapabilityNode]:
        """Retrieves a node by its ID."""
        return self.nodes.get(node_id)

    def connect_nodes(self, node_id1: str, node_id2: str, relationship: str, strength: float = 1.0):
        """Connects two nodes."""
        node1 = self.get_node(node_id1)
        node2 = self.get_node(node_id2)
        if node1 and node2:
            node1.add_connection(node_id2, relationship, strength)
            node2.add_connection(node_id1, relationship, strength)
            logger.info(f"Nodes {node_id1} and {node_id2} connected ({relationship}, strength={strength})")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Cannot connect nodes: {node_id1} or {node_id2} not found.")

    def get_all_nodes(self) -> List[CapabilityNode]:
        """Gets a list of all nodes."""
        return list(self.nodes.values())

    def get_nodes_by_capability(self, capability: str) -> List[CapabilityNode]:
        """Gets nodes that support a specific capability."""
        return [node for node in self.nodes.values() if capability in node.capabilities]

class MemoryGraph:
    """
    Represents the system's memory as a network of nodes and connections.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.graph = nx.DiGraph()

    def add_node(self, node_id: str, metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None):
        """Adds a node to the memory graph."""
        self.graph.add_node(node_id, **(metadata or {}))

    def connect_nodes(self, node_id1: str, node_id2: str, relationship: str, weight: float = 1.0, metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None):
        """Connects two nodes in the memory graph."""
        if self.graph.has_node(node_id1) and self.graph.has_node(node_id2):
            self.graph.add_edge(node_id1, node_id2, relationship=relationship, weight=weight, **(metadata or {}))
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Cannot connect nodes: {node_id1} or {node_id2} not found in memory graph.")

    def get_node_neighbors(self, node_id: str) -> List[str]:
        """Gets the IDs of nodes connected to a given node."""
        if self.graph.has_node(node_id):
            return list(self.graph.neighbors(node_id))
        else:
            return []

    def get_edge_data(self, node_id1: str, node_id2: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Gets data associated with a connection between two nodes."""
        if self.graph.has_edge(node_id1, node_id2):
            return self.graph.get_edge_data(node_id1, node_id2)
        else:
            return None

    def get_graph_snapshot(self) -> nx.DiGraph:
        """Returns a copy of the current memory graph."""
        return self.graph.copy()

class DataPipeline:
    """
    Handles data flow between nodes and modules.
    """

    def __init__(self, max_queue_size: int, concurrency_enabled: bool):
        """
        Args:
            max_queue_size: Maximum size of the data queue.
            concurrency_enabled: Whether concurrent processing is allowed.
        """
        self.max_queue_size = max_queue_size
        self.concurrency_enabled = concurrency_enabled
        self.queue: asyncio.Queue[Any] = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=max_queue_size)
        self.consumers: Dict[str, Callable[[Any], Awaitable[Any]]] = {}  # {consumer_id: consumer_function}
        self.processing_tasks: Dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}  # {consumer_id: processing_task}
        self.is_running = False

    def register_consumer(self, consumer_id: str, consumer_function: Callable[[Any], Awaitable[Any]]):
        """Registers a consumer function for data."""
        if consumer_id not in self.consumers:
            self.consumers[consumer_id] = consumer_function
            logger.info(f"Consumer '{consumer_id}' registered in DataPipeline.")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Consumer '{consumer_id}' already registered.")

    def unregister_consumer(self, consumer_id: str):
        """Unregisters a consumer function."""
        if consumer_id in self.consumers:
            del self.consumers[consumer_id]
            logger.info(f"Consumer '{consumer_id}' unregistered from DataPipeline.")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Consumer '{consumer_id}' not found for unregistration.")

    async def enqueue(self, data: Any):
        """Adds data to the processing queue."""
        await self.queue.put(data)
        logger.debug(f"Data enqueued in DataPipeline (size: {self.queue.qsize()}).")

    async def run(self):
        """Starts the data processing loop."""
        if self.is_running:
            logger.warning("DataPipeline is already running.")
            return

        self.is_running = True
        logger.info("DataPipeline started.")

        try:
            while self.is_running:
                data = await self.queue.get()
                logger.debug(f"Data dequeued from DataPipeline (size: {self.queue.qsize()}).")

                # Process data concurrently or sequentially
                if self.concurrency_enabled:
                    await asyncio.gather(
                        *[self._process_data_with_consumer(consumer_id, consumer_func, data)
                          for consumer_id, consumer_func in self.consumers.items()]
                    )
                else:
                    for consumer_id, consumer_func in self.consumers.items():
                        await self._process_data_with_consumer(consumer_id, consumer_func, data)

                self.queue.task_done()  # Indicate that processing is complete

        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            logger.info("DataPipeline processing loop cancelled.")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error in DataPipeline processing loop: {e}", exc_info=True)
        finally:
            self.is_running = False
            logger.info("DataPipeline stopped.")

    async def _process_data_with_consumer(self, consumer_id: str, consumer_func: Callable[[Any], Awaitable[Any]], data: Any):
        """
        Processes data with a registered consumer function, handling errors and logging.
        """
        try:
            logger.debug(f"Processing data with consumer '{consumer_id}'.")
            await consumer_func(data)
            logger.debug(f"Data processed by consumer '{consumer_id}'.")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error processing data with consumer '{consumer_id}': {e}", exc_info=True)

    async def stop(self):
        """Stops the data processing loop."""
        self.is_running = False
        logger.info("DataPipeline stopping...")
        # Optionally, wait for queue to empty or tasks to complete
        # await self.queue.join()
        # for task in self.processing_tasks.values():
        #     task.cancel()
        # await asyncio.gather(*self.processing_tasks.values(), return_exceptions=True)
        logger.info("DataPipeline stopped.")

Step 2: Define Missing Core Classes

Based on the previous analysis, we need to implement the following core classes to provide the foundation for the system's functionality:

CoreLaws: Defines fundamental rules governing node behavior.
CapabilityNode: Base class for nodes with specific processing capabilities.
NodeManager: Manages the creation, retrieval, and connections of nodes.
MemoryGraph: Represents the system's memory as a network of nodes and connections.
DataPipeline: Handles data flow between nodes and modules.
Here's a possible implementation of these classes:

Python

# core/core_classes.py
import logging
import time
import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Any, Optional, Callable
import networkx as nx
import asyncio

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class CoreLaws:
    """
    Defines fundamental laws governing node behavior and interactions.
    """

    def __init__(self, energy_decay_rate: float = 0.01, connection_strength_decay: float = 0.005):
        """
        Args:
            energy_decay_rate: Rate at which node energy decreases over time.
            connection_strength_decay: Rate at which connection strength decays.
        """
        self.energy_decay_rate = energy_decay_rate
        self.connection_strength_decay = connection_strength_decay

    def apply_energy_decay(self, node: 'CapabilityNode'):
        """Applies energy decay to a node."""
        node.energy = max(0.0, node.energy - self.energy_decay_rate)

    def update_connection_strength(self, connection: Dict[str, Any]):
        """Updates the strength of a connection."""
        connection["strength"] = max(0.0, connection["strength"] - self.connection_strength_decay)


class CapabilityNode:
    """
    Base class for nodes with specific processing capabilities.
    """

    def __init__(self, node_id: str, capabilities: List[str], core_laws: CoreLaws):
        """
        Args:
            node_id: Unique identifier for the node.
            capabilities: List of strings representing the node's processing capabilities.
            core_laws: CoreLaws instance governing node behavior.
        """
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.capabilities = capabilities
        self.core_laws = core_laws
        self.energy = 1.0
        self.connections: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}  # {target_id: {relationship: str, strength: float}}
        self.data_queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()  # Queue for incoming data
        self.last_processed: Optional[float] = None
        self.state: Dict[str, Any] = {}  # Dynamic state information
        self.processing_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None

    async def process_data(self, data: Any) -> Any:
        """
        Processes incoming data using the node's capabilities.
        This is the main method called by the DataPipeline.
        """
        await self.data_queue.put(data)  # Enqueue data
        if self.processing_task is None or self.processing_task.done():
            self.processing_task = asyncio.create_task(self._process_queue())  # Start processing
        return None  # Placeholder

    async def _process_queue(self):
        """
        Internal method to process data from the queue.
        """
        while not self.data_queue.empty():
            data = await self.data_queue.get()
            try:
                result = self.execute_capability(data)
                self.last_processed = time.time()
                self.core_laws.apply_energy_decay(self)  # Apply energy decay
                self._update_connections()  # Update connection strengths
                # ... (Handle result and send to other nodes/modules)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Error processing data in node {self.node_id}: {e}", exc_info=True)
            finally:
                self.data_queue.task_done()

    @abstractmethod
    def execute_capability(self, data: Any) -> Any:
        """
        Executes a specific processing capability on the input data.
        To be implemented by subclasses (e.g., TextNode, VisualNode).
        """
        pass

    def add_connection(self, target_id: str, relationship: str, strength: float = 1.0):
        """Adds a connection to another node."""
        if target_id not in self.connections:
            self.connections[target_id] = {"relationship": relationship, "strength": strength}

    def get_connections(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Gets all connections of the node."""
        return self.connections

    def update_connection_strength(self, target_id: str):
        """Updates the strength of a connection."""
        if target_id in self.connections:
            self.core_laws.update_connection_strength(self.connections[target_id])


class NodeManager:
    """
    Manages the creation, retrieval, and connections of nodes.
    """

    def __init__(self, core_laws: CoreLaws):
        """
        Args:
            core_laws: CoreLaws instance for node governance.
        """
        self.nodes: Dict[str, CapabilityNode] = {}
        self.core_laws = core_laws

    def create_node(self, node_type: str, capabilities: List[str], initial_data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> CapabilityNode:
        """
        Creates a new node.
        """
        node_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        node = CapabilityNode(node_id=node_id, capabilities=capabilities, core_laws=self.core_laws)
        # ... (Initialize node properties based on initial_data)
        self.nodes[node_id] = node
        logger.info(f"Node created: {node_id} ({node_type})")
        return node

    def get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Optional[CapabilityNode]:
        """Retrieves a node by its ID."""
        return self.nodes.get(node_id)

    def connect_nodes(self, node_id1: str, node_id2: str, relationship: str, strength: float = 1.0):
        """Connects two nodes."""
        node1 = self.get_node(node_id1)
        node2 = self.get_node(node_id2)
        if node1 and node2:
            node1.add_connection(node_id2, relationship, strength)
            node2.add_connection(node_id1, relationship, strength)
            logger.info(f"Nodes {node_id1} and {node_id2} connected ({relationship}, strength={strength})")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Cannot connect nodes: {node_id1} or {node_id2} not found.")

    def get_all_nodes(self) -> List[CapabilityNode]:
        """Gets a list of all nodes."""
        return list(self.nodes.values())

    def get_nodes_by_capability(self, capability: str) -> List[CapabilityNode]:
        """Gets nodes that support a specific capability."""
        return [node for node in self.nodes.values() if capability in node.capabilities]


class MemoryGraph:
    """
    Represents the system's memory as a network of nodes and connections.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.graph = nx.DiGraph()

    def add_node(self, node_id: str, metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None):

    def connect_nodes(self, node_id1: str, node_id2: str, relationship: str, weight: float = 1.0, metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None):
        """Connects two nodes in the memory graph."""
        if self.graph.has_node(node_id1) and self.graph.has_node(node_id2):
            self.graph.add_edge(node_id1, node_id2, relationship=relationship, weight=weight, **(metadata or {}))
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Cannot connect nodes: {node_id1} or {node_id2} not found in memory graph.")

    def get_node_neighbors(self, node_id: str) -> List[str]:
        """Gets the IDs of nodes connected to a given node."""
        if self.graph.has_node(node_id):
            return list(self.graph.neighbors(node_id))
        else:
            return []

    def get_edge_data(self, node_id1: str, node_id2: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:

class DataPipeline:
    """
    Handles data flow between nodes and modules.
    """

    def __init__(self, max_queue_size: int, concurrency_enabled: bool):
        """
        Args:
            max_queue_size: Maximum size of the data queue.
            concurrency_enabled: Whether concurrent processing is allowed.
        """
        self.max_queue_size = max_queue_size
        self.concurrency_enabled = concurrency_enabled

        self.processing_tasks: Dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}  # {consumer_id: processing_task}
        self.is_running = False

    def register_consumer(self, consumer_id: str, consumer_function: Callable[[Any], Awaitable[Any]]):
        """Registers a consumer function for data."""
        if consumer_id not in self.consumers:
            self.consumers[consumer_id] = consumer_function
            logger.info(f"Consumer '{consumer_id}' registered in DataPipeline.")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Consumer '{consumer_id}' already registered.")

    def unregister_consumer(self, consumer_id: str):
        """Unregisters a consumer function."""
        if consumer_id in self.consumers:
            del self.consumers[consumer_id]
            logger.info(f"Consumer '{consumer_id}' unregistered from DataPipeline.")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Consumer '{consumer_id}' not found for unregistration.")

        """Adds data to the processing queue."""
        await self.queue.put(data)
        logger.debug(f"Data enqueued in DataPipeline (size: {self.queue.qsize()}).")

    async def run(self):

            return

                    )

                    for consumer_id, consumer_func in self.consumers.items():
                        await self._process_data_with_consumer(consumer_id, consumer_func, data)

                self.queue.task_done()  # Indicate that processing is complete

        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            logger.info("DataPipeline processing loop cancelled.")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error in DataPipeline processing loop: {e}", exc_info=True)
        finally:
            self.is_running = False
            logger.info("DataPipeline stopped.")

    async def _process_data_with_consumer(self, consumer_id: str, consumer_func: Callable[[Any], Awaitable[Any]], data: Any):
        """
        Processes data with a registered consumer function, handling errors and logging.
        """

            logger.debug(f"Processing data with consumer '{consumer_id}'.")

            logger.debug(f"Data processed by consumer '{consumer_id}'.")

Asynchronous Processing: The DataPipeline uses asyncio for efficient, non-blocking data flow.

Step 3: Implement Node Types

Here's how we can define the TextNode, VisualNode, and DataSimulationNode. These classes will inherit from the CapabilityNode base class and implement the execute_capability method to perform their specific processing tasks.

Python

# nodes/nodes.py
import logging
from typing import List, Dict, Any
import numpy as np
import asyncio
from core.core_classes import CapabilityNode, CoreLaws  # Assuming core_classes.py is in the same directory

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class TextNode(CapabilityNode):
    """
    Node for processing and analyzing text data.
    """

    def __init__(self, node_id: str, core_laws: CoreLaws, llm_service):
        super().__init__(node_id, capabilities=["text_analysis", "text_generation"], core_laws=core_laws)
        self.llm_service = llm_service
        self.state: Dict[str, Any] = {}  # Example: Store dialogue history, etc.

    async def execute_capability(self, data: Any) -> Any:
        """
        Executes text-related capabilities.
        """
        if isinstance(data, str):
            return await self._process_text(data)
        elif isinstance(data, dict) and "query" in data:
            return await self._query_llm(data["query"])
        else:
            logger.warning(f"TextNode: Unsupported data type: {type(data)}")
            return {"error": "Unsupported data type"}

    async def _process_text(self, text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Analyzes and extracts information from text.
        """
        # Placeholder: Implement real text analysis using LLM or other libraries
        # Replace this with your actual logic
        logger.info(f"TextNode processing text: {text[:50]}...")
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)  # Simulate processing time

        # Example: Simple text splitting (replace with actual NLP)
        words = text.split()
        entities = [(word, "WORD") for word in words[:5]]  # Dummy entities
        sentiment = 0.5  # Placeholder
        summary = f"Summary of the text: {text[:20]}..."

        return {
            "type": "text_analysis",
            "words": words,
            "entities": entities,
            "sentiment": sentiment,
            "summary": summary,
            "processed_by": self.node_id
        }

    async def _query_llm(self, query: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Queries the LLM for text generation or complex analysis.
        """
        if self.llm_service.is_ready():
            logger.info(f"TextNode querying LLM with: {query[:50]}...")
            llm_response = self.llm_service.generate([LLMMessage(role="user", content=query)])
            if llm_response:
                return llm_response.content
            else:
                return "LLM returned no response."
        else:
            logger.warning("LLMService not ready.")
            return "LLMService not available."

class DataSimulationNode(CapabilityNode):
    """
    Node for simulating data (e.g., for testing or modeling).
    """

    def __init__(self, node_id: str, core_laws: CoreLaws):
        super().__init__(node_id, capabilities=["data_simulation"], core_laws=core_laws)
        self.state: Dict[str, Any] = {}  # Example: Store simulation parameters

    async def execute_capability(self, data: Any) -> Any:
        """

        """

            return await self._simulate_data(data["type"], data.get("params", {}))
        else:

    async def _simulate_data(self, data_type: str, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[float]:

        # Replace this with your actual logic

            end = params.get("end", 10)
            return list(range(start, end))
        else:
            return [0.0] * 5  # Default: 5 zeros

"""
Kaleidoscope AI - Task-Driven Orchestrator
=======================================
This script integrates the task management system with node-based processing.
It defines tasks that are executed by the appropriate nodes,
leveraging the LLM service for text-based analysis.

    ]
)

    return result

# --- Main Orchestration ---

async def main():
    """
    Main function to orchestrate the AI system.
    """

    # Initialize components
    logger.info("Initializing components...")
    node_manager = NodeManager()
    perspective_manager = PerspectiveManager()
    llm_processor = GPTProcessor()
    task_scheduler = OptimizedTaskScheduler()

    # Create nodes
    text_node = TextNode(node_id="text_node_1", core_laws=None, llm_processor=llm_processor)  # Placeholder CoreLaws

    node_manager.register_node(text_node)
    node_manager.register_node(visual_node)

    # Example: Schedule tasks
    logger.info("Scheduling tasks...")

    # Task 1: LLM-based code analysis
    code_analysis_task_id = task_scheduler.add_task(
        name="Analyze Code",
        func=analyze_code_with_llm,
        args=[llm_processor, "Sample code snippet"],
        priority=TaskPriority.HIGH,
        estimated_resources={"cpu_percent": 70.0, "memory_percent": 30.0}
    )

    # Task 2: Text processing with TextNode
    text_processing_task_id = task_scheduler.add_task(
        name="Process User Input",
        func=process_text_with_node,
        args=[text_node, "User query", "analyze"],
        priority=TaskPriority.NORMAL,
        estimated_resources={"cpu_percent": 50.0, "memory_percent": 20.0}
    )

    # Task 3: Image processing with VisualNode
    image_processing_task_id = task_scheduler.add_task(
        name="Process Image",
        func=process_image_with_node,
        args=[visual_node, b"image_data"],
        priority=TaskPriority.LOW,
        estimated_resources={"cpu_percent": 60.0, "memory_percent": 40.0}
    )

    # Run tasks
    logger.info("Running tasks...")
    await task_scheduler.run()

    # Retrieve and process results
    logger.info("Retrieving results...")
    code_analysis_result = task_scheduler.get_task_result(code_analysis_task_id)
    text_processing_result = task_scheduler.get_task_result(text_processing_task_id)
    image_processing_result = task_scheduler.get_task_result(image_processing_task_id)

    if code_analysis_result and code_analysis_result.success:
        print("Code Analysis Result:", code_analysis_result.data)
    if text_processing_result and text_processing_result.success:
        print("Text Processing Result:", text_processing_result.data)
    if image_processing_result and image_processing_result.success:
        print("Image Processing Result:", image_processing_result.data)

    # Cleanup
    await task_scheduler.shutdown()
    logger.info("System shutdown.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

# core/core_classes.py
import logging
import time
import uuid
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Any, Optional, Callable
import networkx as nx
import asyncio

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class EntropyPool:
    """
    High-quality entropy source for quantum-inspired operations
    using system entropy sources when available and novel hybridization
    techniques to maximize unpredictability.
    """
    def __init__(self, seed=None):
        self.random_gen = random.Random(seed if seed is not None else int(time.time()))
        self.sys_entropy_available = False
        self._initialize_entropy_sources()

    def _initialize_entropy_sources(self):
        """Gather entropy from various system sources."""
        try:
            entropy_bytes = b''

            # System timer entropy (cross-platform)
            timer_bytes = b''
            for _ in range(16):
                start = time.perf_counter_ns()
                time.sleep(0.0001)  # Small sleep
                end = time.perf_counter_ns()
                timer_bytes += struct.pack('!Q', end - start)

            entropy_bytes += timer_bytes

            # Try socket entropy (cross-platform)
            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
                s.settimeout(0.1)
                try:
                    s.connect(('8.8.8.8', 80))
                except (socket.timeout, socket.error):
                    pass
                entropy_bytes += str(s.getsockname()[0]).encode() + struct.pack('!H', s.getsockname()[1])
                s.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Process OS entropy sources
            try:
                entropy_bytes += str(os.getpid()).encode()
                entropy_bytes += str(os.getloadavg()).encode()
            except:
                pass

            # If we got entropy, use it to seed
            if entropy_bytes:
                entropy_hash = hashlib.sha512(entropy_bytes).digest()
                entropy_int = int.from_bytes(entropy_hash, byteorder='big')
                self.random_gen = random.Random(entropy_int ^ (self.random_gen.randint(0, 2**64 - 1)))
                self.sys_entropy_available = True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Warning: Using reduced entropy: {e}")

    def get_float(self, min_val=0.0, max_val=1.0):
        """Get a random float in the specified range."""
        return self.random_gen.uniform(min_val, max_val)

    def get_int(self, min_val=0, max_val=100):
        """Get a random integer in the specified range."""
        return self.random_gen.randint(min_val, max_val)

    def get_bytes(self, length=32):
        """Get random bytes of specified length."""
        return bytes(self.random_gen.getrandbits(8) for _ in range(length))

    def refresh(self):
        """Refresh entropy from system sources."""
        time_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
        current_state = self.random_gen.getstate()
        self.random_gen.seed(hash((str(current_state), time_ns)))


class Tensor:
    """
    Optimized multidimensional array implementation
    without numpy dependency.
    Supports CP and Tucker-like
    decompositions using custom optimization algorithms.
    """
    def __init__(self, shape, data=None):
        self.shape = shape
        self.ndim = len(shape)
        self.size = 1
        for d in shape:
            self.size *= d

        if data is None:
            self.data = [0.0] * self.size
        else:
            if len(data) != self.size:
                raise ValueError(f"Data size {len(data)} doesn't match tensor size {self.size}")
            self.data = list(data)

    def _index(self, indices):
        """Convert multidimensional indices to flat index."""
        if len(indices) != self.ndim:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected {self.ndim} indices, got {len(indices)}")

        idx = 0
        stride = 1
        for i in range(self.ndim - 1, -1, -1):
            idx += indices[i] * stride
            stride *= self.shape[i]
        return idx

    def __getitem__(self, indices):
        """Get value at specified indices."""
        return self.data[self._index(indices)]

    def __setitem__(self, indices, value):
        """Set value at specified indices."""
        self.data[self._index(indices)] = value

    def copy(self):
        """Return a copy of this tensor."""
        return Tensor(self.shape, copy.deepcopy(self.data))

    def zeros_like(self):
        """Create zero-filled tensor of same shape."""
        return Tensor(self.shape)

    def randn(self, scale=1.0, entropy_pool=None):
        """Fill tensor with random values."""
        if entropy_pool is None:
            entropy_pool = EntropyPool()

        for i in range(len(self.data)):
            # Box-Muller transform for normal distribution
            u1 = entropy_pool.get_float(0.0001, 0.9999)
            u2 = entropy_pool.get_float(0.0001, 0.9999)
            z0 = math.sqrt(-2.0 * math.log(u1)) * math.cos(2.0 * math.pi * u2)
            self.data[i] = z0 * scale

        return self

    def CP_decompose(self, rank, max_iter=100):
        """
        Canonical Polyadic Decomposition (CP/PARAFAC) without numpy
        Returns list of factor matrices
        """
        if rank <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Rank must be positive")

        # Initialize factor matrices randomly
        entropy = EntropyPool()
        factors = []
        for dim_size in self.shape:
            factor = [[entropy.get_float(-0.01, 0.01) for _ in range(rank)] for _ in range(dim_size)]
            # Normalize columns
            for r in range(rank):
                norm = math.sqrt(sum(factor[i][r]**2 for i in range(dim_size)))
                if norm > 0:
                    for i in range(dim_size):
                        factor[i][r] /= norm
            factors.append(factor)

        # Alternating Least Squares
        for iteration in range(max_iter):
            for mode in range(self.ndim):
                # Skip update if dimension is 1
                if self.shape[mode] == 1:
                    continue

                # Khatri-Rao products and mode-n unfolding
                # This is simplified for memory efficiency
                for i in range(self.shape[mode]):
                    for r in range(rank):
                        # Update rule for factor element
                        factors[mode][i][r] = self._update_factor_element(factors, mode, i, r)

                # Normalize columns after update
                for r in range(rank):
                    norm = math.sqrt(sum(factors[mode][i][r]**2 for i in range(self.shape[mode])))
                    if norm > 0:
                        for i in range(self.shape[mode]):
                            factors[mode][i][r] /= norm

        return factors

    def _update_factor_element(self, factors, mode, i, r):
        """
        Helper for CP decomposition - updates a single factor matrix element
        using an efficient computation approach that avoids large intermediate tensors
        """
        # Calculate update for factors[mode][i][r]
        # For each element of the tensor that includes factors[mode][i][r]
        result = 0.0
        contribution = 0.0

        # Generate indices for each element
        indices = [0] * self.ndim

        # Batch computation with strided sampling to avoid full combinatorial
        # This is an approximation technique to make it computationally feasible
        batch_size = min(1000, self.size)
        for _ in range(batch_size):
            # Generate random indices except for mode
            for m in range(self.ndim):
                if m != mode:
                    indices[m] = random.randint(0, self.shape[m] - 1)

            # Use the current index for mode
            indices[mode] = i

            # Get tensor value
            tensor_val = self[tuple(indices)]

            # Compute product of all factors except mode for this rank
            product = 1.0
            for m in range(self.ndim):
                if m != mode:
                    product *= factors[m][indices[m]][r]

            result += tensor_val * product
            contribution += product * product

        # Scale by batch_size ratio
        scaling = self.size / batch_size
        result *= scaling
        contribution *= scaling

        if contribution > 0:
            return result / contribution
        return 0.0

    def to_dict(self):
        """Convert tensor to dictionary representation for serialization."""
        return {
            "shape": self.shape,
            "data": self.data
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data_dict):
        """Create tensor from dictionary representation."""
        return cls(data_dict["shape"], data_dict["data"])

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Tensor(shape={self.shape}, size={self.size})"


class NodeState:
    """
    Represents the state of a quantum node in the complex system.
    Each node has dynamic properties including:
    - Features vector
    - Position in space
    - Energy level
    - Stability factor
    - Connection graph
    """
    def __init__(self, node_id=None, features=None, position=None,
                 energy=0.5, stability=0.8, metadata=None):
        self.id = node_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.features = features or []
        self.position = position or [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]
        self.energy = max(0.0, min(1.0, energy))
        self.stability = max(0.0, min(1.0, stability))
        self.connections = {}  # {node_id: strength}
        self.metadata = metadata or {}
        self.createdAt = Date.now()
        self.lastUpdated = Date.now()

    def to_dict(self):
        """Convert state to a dictionary for serialization."""
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "features": self.features,
            "position": self.position,
            "energy": self.energy,
            "stability": self.stability,
            "connections": self.connections,
            "metadata": self.metadata,
            "createdAt": self.createdAt,
            "lastUpdated": self.lastUpdated
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        """Create a NodeState instance from a dictionary."""
        node = cls(node_id=data.get("id", str(uuid.uuid4())))
        node.features = data.get("features", [])
        node.position = data.get("position", [0.0, 0.0, 0.0])
        node.energy = data.get("energy", 0.5)
        node.stability = data.get("stability", 0.8)
        node.connections = data.get("connections", {})
        node.metadata = data.get("metadata", {})
        node.createdAt = data.get("createdAt", Date.now())
        node.lastUpdated = data.get("lastUpdated", Date.now())
        return node


class QuantumEngine:
    """
    Core quantum-inspired engine that implements:
    - Feature extraction through tensor decomposition
    - Dynamic graph evolution with stress and tension
    - Multidimensional pattern analysis
    """
    def __init__(self, dimension=64, data_dir="./data"):
        self.dimension = dimension
        self.data_dir = data_dir
        self.nodes = {}  # {node_id: NodeState}
        self.pending_connections = []  # [(src, dst, strength)]
        self.entropy_source = EntropyPool()
        self.last_mutation_time = Date.now()
        self.mutation_interval = 10.0  # seconds
        self.speculative_insights = []

        # Ensure data directory exists
        os.makedirs(data_dir, exist_ok=True)

        print(f"Quantum Engine initialized with dimension {dimension}")

        # Load existing data if available
        self._load_state()

    def _load_state(self):
        """Load engine state from disk."""
        state_file = os.path.join(self.data_dir, "quantum_state.json")
        if os.path.exists(state_file):
            try:
                with open(state_file, 'r') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)

                # Load nodes
                nodes_data = data.get("nodes", {})
                for node_id, node_data in nodes_data.items():
                    self.nodes[node_id] = NodeState.from_dict(node_data)

                # Load pending connections
                self.pending_connections = data.get("pending_connections", [])

                # Load speculative insights
                self.speculative_insights = data.get("speculative_insights", [])

                print(f"Loaded {len(self.nodes)} nodes and {len(self.speculative_insights)} insights from {state_file}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error loading state: {e}")
                # Start fresh
                self.nodes = {}
                self.pending_connections = []
                self.speculative_insights = []

    def _save_state(self):
        """Save engine state to disk."""
        state_file = os.path.join(self.data_dir, "quantum_state.json")
        try:
            data = {
                "nodes": {node_id: node.to_dict() for node_id, node in self.nodes.items()},
                "pending_connections": self.pending_connections,
                "speculative_insights": self.speculative_insights
            }

            with open(state_file, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

            print(f"Saved state with {len(self.nodes)} nodes to {state_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error saving state: {e}")

    def create_node(self, properties = {}):
        if (Object.keys(this.nodes).length >= this.config.maxNodes) {
          console.warn(`Maximum node limit (${this.config.maxNodes}) reached.`);
          return null;
        }
    
        // Generate unique ID
        const nodeId = `node-<span class="math-inline">\{Date\.now\(\)\}\-</span>{Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`;
    
        // Set default properties
        const nodeData = {
          id: nodeId,
          position: properties.position || new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0),
          energy: properties.energy !== undefined ? properties.energy : this.config.energyLevel,
          phase: properties.phase !== undefined ? properties.phase : Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
          stability: properties.stability !== undefined ? properties.stability : 0.8,
          connections: new Set(),
          metadata: properties.metadata || {},
          createdAt: Date.now(),
          lastUpdated: Date.now()
        };
    
        // Calculate node size based on energy
        const nodeSize = 0.2 + nodeData.energy * 0.8;
    
        // Create node geometry
        const geometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(nodeSize, 16, 16);
    
        // Create node material
        const material = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({
          color: this._getNodeColor(nodeData.energy, nodeData.phase),
          emissive: new THREE.Color(this._getNodeColor(nodeData.energy, nodeData.phase)).multiplyScalar(0.3),
          transparent: true,
          opacity: 0.8,
          wireframe: this.config.wireframe
        });
    
        // Create mesh
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
        mesh.position.copy(nodeData.position);
        mesh.castShadow = true;
        mesh.receiveShadow = true;
    
        // Set custom properties on mesh
        mesh.userData.id = nodeId;
        mesh.userData.type = 'node';
    
        // Add to scene
        this.nodesGroup.add(mesh);
    
        // Store node data and mesh
        this.nodes.set(nodeId, {
          mesh: mesh,
          data: nodeData
        });
    
        // Update stats
        this.stats.nodes = this.nodes.size;
        this._updateStatsDisplay();
    
        return nodeId;
      }
    
    def remove_node(self, nodeId):
        if (!this.nodes.has(nodeId)) {
          console.warn(`Node ${nodeId} does not exist`);
          return false;
        }
    
        // Get node
        const node = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
    
        // Remove from scene
        this.nodesGroup.remove(node.mesh);
    
        // Remove any connections to this node
        for (const otherNodeId of node.data.connections) {
          // Get connection ID
          const connId = [nodeId, otherNodeId].sort().join('_');
    
          // Remove connection
          if (this.connections.has(connId)) {
            const connection = this.connections.get(connId);
            this.connectionsGroup.remove(connection.line);
            this.connections.delete(connId);
          }
    
          // Update other node's connections
          if (this.nodes.has(otherNodeId)) {
            this.nodes.get(otherNodeId).data.connections.delete(nodeId);
          }
        }
    
        // Remove node
        this.nodes.delete(nodeId);
    
        // Update selected node if it was removed
        if (this.selectedNode === nodeId) {
          this.selectedNode = null;
          this._hideNodeDetails();
        }
    
        // Update hovered node if it was removed
        if (this.hoveredNode === nodeId) {
          this.hoveredNode = null;
        }
    
        // Update entangled nodes if it was in the set
        if (this.entangledNodes.has(nodeId)) {
          this.entangledNodes.delete(nodeId);
        }
    
        // Update stats
        this.stats.nodes = this.nodes.size;
        this._updateStatsDisplay();
    
        this._showToast(`Node removed: ${nodeId.substring(0, 8)}...`);
        return true;
      }
    
    def create_connection(self, nodeId1, nodeId2, strength = 0.5):
        if (this.connections.size >= this.config.maxConnections) {
          console.warn(`Maximum connection limit (${this.config.maxConnections}) reached.`);
          return null;
        }
    
        // Check if both nodes exist
        if (!this.nodes.has(nodeId1) || !this.nodes.has(nodeId2)) {
          console.warn("Cannot create connection - one or both nodes don't exist");
          return null;
        }
    
        // Check if it's not a self-connection
        if (nodeId1 === nodeId2) {
          console.warn("Cannot create self-connection");
          return null;
        }
    
        // Generate connection ID
        const connId = [nodeId1, nodeId2].sort().join('_');
    
        // Check if connection already exists
        if (this.connections.has(connId)) {
          return connId;
        }
    
        // Get node data
        const node1 = this.nodes.get(nodeId1);
        const node2 = this.nodes.get(nodeId2);
    
        // Create connection properties
        const connectionData = {
          id: connId,
          sourceId: nodeId1,
          targetId: nodeId2,
          strength: strength,
          createdAt: Date.now(),
          lastUpdated: Date.now()
        };
    
        // Add connection to nodes
        node1.data.connections.add(nodeId2);
        node2.data.connections.add(nodeId1);
    
        // Calculate connection color
        const connectionColor = new THREE.Color(this.config.nodeColor)
          .lerp(new THREE.Color(0xffffff), strength * 0.5);
    
        // Create line geometry
        const points = [
          node1.mesh.position.clone(),
          node2.mesh.position.clone()
        ];
        const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
    
        // Create line material
        const material = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
          color: connectionColor,
          transparent: true,
          opacity: strength * 0.7,
          linewidth: 1
        });
    
        // Create line
        const line = new THREE.Line(geometry, material);
    
        // Set custom properties on line
        line.userData.id = connId;
        line.userData.type = 'connection';
    
        // Add to scene
        this.connectionsGroup.add(line);
    
        // Store connection data and line
        this.connections.set(connId, {
          line: line,
          data: connectionData
        });
    
        // Update stats
        this.stats.connections = this.connections.size;
        this._updateStatsDisplay();
    
        return connId;
      }
    
    def remove_connection(self, connId):
        if (!this.connections.has(connId)) {
          console.warn(`Connection ${connId} does not exist`);
          return false;
        }
    
        // Get connection
        const connection = this.connections.get(connId);
    
        // Get node IDs
        const [nodeId1, nodeId2] = connId.split('_');
    
        // Remove from scene
        this.connectionsGroup.remove(connection.line);
    
        // Update nodes
        if (this.nodes.has(nodeId1)) {
          this.nodes.get(nodeId1).data.connections.delete(nodeId2);
        }
    
        if (this.nodes.has(nodeId2)) {
          this.nodes.get(nodeId2).data.connections.delete(nodeId1);
        }
    
        // Remove connection
        this.connections.delete(connId);
    
        // Update stats
        this.stats.connections = this.connections.size;
        this._updateStatsDisplay();
    
        return true;
      }
    
    def clear_all():
        // Remove all connections
        for (const [connId, connection] of this.connections.entries()) {
          this.connectionsGroup.remove(connection.line);
        }
        this.connections.clear();
    
        // Remove all nodes
        for (const [nodeId, node] of this.nodes.entries()) {
          this.nodesGroup.remove(node.mesh);
        }
        this.nodes.clear();
    
        // Clear selection
        this.selectedNode = null;
        this.hoveredNode = null;
        this.entangledNodes.clear();
    
        // Update stats
        this.stats.nodes = 0;
        this.stats.connections = 0;
        this._updateStatsDisplay();
    
        this._showToast("Cleared all nodes and connections");
        return true;
      }
    
    def process_text(text):
        if (!text || text.trim() === '') return null;
    
        // Hash the text for deterministic output
        const hash = this._hashString(text);
    
        // Create metadata
        const metadata = {
          type: "text",
          content: text.length > 50 ? text.substring(0, 50) + "..." : text,
          timestamp: Date.now()
        };
    
        // Calculate a deterministic position based on hash
        const position = new THREE.Vector3(
          (hash[0] / 255) * 2 - 1 * this.config.cubeSize / 2,
          (hash[1] / 255) * 2 - 1 * this.config.cubeSize / 2,
          (hash[2] / 255) * 2 - 1 * this.config.cubeSize / 2
        );
    
        // Calculate energy and phase based on text
        const energy = 0.3 + (text.length % 100) / 100 * 0.7;
        const phase = (hash[3] / 255) * Math.PI * 2;
    
        // Create node
        const nodeId = this.createNode({
          position: position,
          energy: energy,
          phase: phase,
          stability: 0.7 + (hash[4] / 255) * 0.3,
          metadata: metadata
        });
    
        if (!nodeId) return null;
    
        // Find close nodes to connect with
        this._connectToNearbyNodes(nodeId, 3);
    
        // Create quantum burst effect
        this._createQuantumBurst(position, 5);
    
        // Update charts
        this._updateChartsDisplay();
    
        this._showToast(`Text processed: ${text.length} characters`);
        return nodeId;
      }
    
    def add_node_at(position, properties = {}):
        // Create node with specified position
        const nodeId = this.createNode({
          position: position,
          energy: properties.energy !== undefined ? properties.energy : this.config.energyLevel,
          phase: properties.phase !== undefined ? properties.phase : Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
          stability: properties.stability !== undefined ? properties.stability : 0.8,
          metadata: properties.metadata || {}
        });
    
        if (nodeId) {
          this._showToast(`Node created: ${nodeId.substring(0, 8)}...`);
          return nodeId;
        }
    
        return null;
      }
    
    def simulate_quantum_analysis(text):
        // Update quantum states based on text
        const hash = this._hashString(text);
    
        for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(hash.length, this.quantumStates.length); i++) {
          const state = this.quantumStates[i];
          state.amplitude = hash[i] / 255;
          state.phase = (hash[i] / 255) * Math.PI * 2;
        }
    
        // Update wave function
        for (let i = 0; i < this.waveFunction.length; i++) {
          let sum = 0;
          for (let j = 0; j < Math.min(5, this.quantumStates.length); j++) {
            const state = this.quantumStates[j];
            const x = (i / this.waveFunction.length) * Math.PI * 4;
            sum += state.amplitude * Math.sin(x * state.frequency + state.phase);
          }
    
          this.waveFunction[i] = sum;
        }
    
        // Return simulated analysis result
        return {
          sentiment: Math.random() * 2 - 1, // -1 to 1
          complexity: 0.1 + (text.length / 1000) * 0.9, // 0.1 to 1
          coherence: hash[0] / 255, // 0 to 1
          quantumEntropy: hash[1] / 255, // 0 to 1
          dominantFrequency: 1 + (hash[2] / 25), // 1 to ~11 Hz
          phaseCoupling: hash[3] / 255 // 0 to 1
        };
      }
    
    def generate_quantum_insights(text):
        const analysis = this.simulateQuantumAnalysis(text);
    
        // Generate insights based on analysis
        const insights = [];
    
        if (analysis.sentiment > 0.5) {
          insights.push("High positive quantum resonance detected in semantic field");
        } else if (analysis.sentiment < -0.5) {
          insights.push("Strong negative phase interference in conceptual structures");
        }
    
        if (analysis.complexity > 0.7) {
          insights.push("Complex multi-dimensional semantic entanglement present");
        } else if (analysis.complexity < 0.3) {
          insights.push("Simple harmonic information patterns observed");
        }
    
        if (analysis.coherence > 0.8) {
          insights.push("Exceptionally coherent quantum information state");
        } else if (analysis.coherence < 0.2) {
          insights.push("Significant decoherence in semantic superposition");
        }
    
        if (analysis.quantumEntropy > 0.7) {
          insights.push("High entropy quantum state suggests creative potential");
        } else if (analysis.quantumEntropy < 0.3) {
          insights.push("Low entropy indicates structured information patterns");
        }
    
        if (analysis.dominantFrequency > 8) {
          insights.push("High frequency semantic oscillations detected");
        } else if (analysis.dominantFrequency < 3) {
          insights.push("Low frequency conceptual resonance observed");
        }
    
        return insights;
      }
    
    def _connectToNearbyNodes(nodeId, maxConnections = 3):
        if (!this.nodes.has(nodeId)) return;
    
        const node = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
        const nodePos = node.data.position;
    
        // Calculate distances to all other nodes
        const distances = [];
        for (const [otherNodeId, otherNode] of this.nodes.entries()) {
          if (otherNodeId !== nodeId) {
            const distance = nodePos.distanceTo(otherNode.data.position);
            distances.push({
              id: otherNodeId,
              distance: distance
            });
          }
        }
    
        // Sort by distance
        distances.sort((a, b) => a.distance - b.distance);
    
        // Connect to closest nodes
        const connectCount = Math.min(maxConnections, distances.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < connectCount; i++) {
          const otherNodeId = distances[i].id;
          const distance = distances[i].distance;
    
          // Only connect if within threshold
          if (distance < this.config.connectionThreshold * 1.5) {
            // Connection strength inversely proportional to distance
            const strength = Math.max(0.2, 1 - (distance / (this.config.connectionThreshold * 1.5)));
            this.createConnection(nodeId, otherNodeId, strength);
          }
        }
      }
    
    def _createQuantumBurst(position, numParticles = 10):
        const size = this.config.cubeSize / 4;
        const particleSize = 0.2;
        const duration = 1.0;
        const particles = [];
    
        // Create particles
        for (let i = 0; i < numParticles; i++) {
          const direction = new THREE.Vector3(
            Math.random() * 2 - 1,
            Math.random() * 2 - 1,
            Math.random() * 2 - 1
          ).normalize();
    
          const velocity = direction.clone().multiplyScalar(size * 2);
    
          const geometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(particleSize, 8, 8);
          const material = new THREE.MeshPhongMaterial({
            color: this.config.nodeColor,
            emissive: this.config.nodeColor,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.5,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 1.0
          });
    
          const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
          mesh.position.copy(position);
    
          this.scene.add(mesh);
    
          particles.push({
            mesh: mesh,
            velocity: velocity,
            startTime: this.simulationTime
          });
        }
    
        // Animate particles
        const animateParticles = () => {
          let allDone = true;
    
          for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
            const particle = particles[i];
            const elapsed = this.simulationTime - particle.startTime;
    
            if (elapsed < duration) {
              allDone = false;
    
              // Update position
              particle.mesh.position.add(
                particle.velocity.clone().multiplyScalar(0.03)
              );
    
              // Update scale and opacity
              const progress = elapsed / duration;
              const scale = 1.0 - progress;
              particle.mesh.scale.set(scale, scale, scale);
              particle.mesh.material.opacity = 1.0 - progress;
            } else if (particle.mesh.parent) {
              // Remove completed particle
              this.scene.remove(particle.mesh);
            }
          }
    
          if (!allDone) {
            requestAnimationFrame(animateParticles);
          }
        };
    
        // Start animation
        requestAnimationFrame(animateParticles);
      }
    
    def _hashString(str):
        let hash = new Uint8Array(16);
    
        for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
