# core/BaseNode.py
import logging
import random
import uuid
import json
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class BaseNode:
    """
    Enhanced BaseNode with "string" properties to connect to other nodes,
    and methods to simulate stress-based adaptation.
    """

    class CoreState:
        def __init__(self):
            self.energy = random.uniform(20, 50)
            self.ready_to_replicate = False
            self.dna = json.dumps({"genes": ["A", "C", "G", "T"], "traits": {"size": 1.0, "speed": 1.0}})
            self.metadata = {"created_at": str(uuid.uuid4()), "memory": []}

    def __init__(self, node_id=None, core_laws=None, node_type="base"):
        self.id = node_id if node_id else str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.core_laws = core_laws
        self.state = BaseNode.CoreState()
        self.node_type = node_type
        self.connections = {}  # {other_node_id: {"relationship": str, "strength": float, "stress": float}}

    def apply_external_stress(self, stress_signal):
        """
        Simulates how an external "stress" signal affects the node.
        This is a basic example; you can make it more complex.
        """
        self.state.energy -= stress_signal * 0.1  # Example: Stress reduces energy
        # You could also imagine stress affecting replication, mutation, etc.

    def add_connection(self, other_node_id, relationship, strength=1.0):
        """
        Adds a connection with a "relationship" type and initial "strength".
        Also initializes "stress" to 0.
        """
        self.connections[other_node_id] = {"relationship": relationship, "strength": strength, "stress": 0.0}

    def update_connection_stress(self, other_node_id, stress_delta):
        """
        Updates the "stress" level on a connection, simulating how external
        factors change the relationship between nodes (Cube principle).
        """
        if other_node_id in self.connections:
            self.connections[other_node_id]["stress"] += stress_delta
            # You might also update the connection "strength" based on stress here

    def process_data(self, data):
        """Placeholder for data processing logic."""
        # ... data processing logic ...
        return {"status": "success", "processed_data": data}  # Placeholder

# core/GrowthLaws.py
import logging
import random
import math
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class GrowthLaws:
    """
    Simulates how "stress" and other factors influence node behavior,
    drawing inspiration from the Cube's dynamic string concept.
    """

    def __init__(self,
                 energy_threshold=100,
                 replication_chance=0.1,
                 base_stress=1.0,  # Base level of environmental stress
                 stress_sensitivity=0.1,  # How much nodes react to stress
                 energy_gain_range=(5, 15)):  # Range for random energy gain
        self.energy_threshold = energy_threshold
        self.replication_chance = replication_chance
        self.base_stress = base_stress
        self.stress_sensitivity = stress_sensitivity
        self.energy_gain_range = energy_gain_range

    def apply(self, nodes, external_stress):
        """
        Applies growth laws, now incorporating external stress.
        """
        for node in nodes:
            self._simulate_energy_dynamics(node, external_stress)
            self._check_replication(node, external_stress)
            # ... other logic ...

    def _simulate_energy_dynamics(self, node, external_stress):
        """
        Energy gain/decay influenced by external stress.
        """
        # "Stress" modulates energy gain (Cube principle)
        effective_stress = self.base_stress + external_stress * self.stress_sensitivity
        gain = random.uniform(self.energy_gain_range[0], self.energy_gain_range[1]) * effective_stress
        node.state.energy += gain

        # Energy decay (simplified)
        node.state.energy -= 1
        node.state.energy = max(0, node.state.energy)

    def _check_replication(self, node, external_stress):
        """
        Replication chance also modulated by stress.
        """
        adaptive_replication_chance = self.replication_chance * (node.state.energy / self.energy_threshold) * external_stress
        if random.random() < adaptive_replication_chance:
            node.state.ready_to_replicate = True
        else:
            node.state.ready_to_replicate = False

    def mutate(self):
        """Placeholder for mutation logic."""
        # Implement mutation logic here (e.g., changing node properties)
        pass

# core/NodeManager.py
import logging
import heapq
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class NodeManager:
    """
    Enhanced NodeManager that manages nodes and their connections,
    including methods to apply stress and simulate network-wide effects.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes: Dict[str, Any] = {}  # {node_id: node_object}
        self.priority_queue = []  # Placeholder for task prioritization
        self.memory_graph = None  # Placeholder for memory graph integration

    def register_node(self, node):
        """Registers a node with the manager."""
        if node.id not in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node.id] = node
            logger.info(f"Registered node: {node.id} ({node.node_type})")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Node ID already exists: {node.id}")

    def unregister_node(self, node_id: str):
        """Unregisters a node from the manager."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            del self.nodes[node_id]
            logger.info(f"Unregistered node: {node_id}")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Node ID not found: {node_id}")

    def get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Retrieves a node by its ID."""
        return self.nodes.get(node_id)

    def get_node_count(self) -> int:
        """Gets the total number of registered nodes."""
        return len(self.nodes)

    def get_all_statuses(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Gets the status of all registered nodes."""
        statuses = {}
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            if hasattr(node, 'state'):
                statuses[node_id] = {"energy": node.state.energy, "status": "active"}  # Example
            else:
                statuses[node_id] = {"status": "unknown"}
        return statuses

    def find_nodes_by_type(self, node_type: str) -> List[Any]:
        """Finds nodes of a specific type."""
        return [node for node in self.nodes.values() if node.node_type == node_type]

    def connect_nodes(self, node_id1, node_id2, relationship, strength=1.0):
        """
        Connects two nodes, creating a "string" relationship.
        """
        if node_id1 in self.nodes and node_id2 in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node_id1].add_connection(node_id2, relationship, strength)
            self.nodes[node_id2].add_connection(node_id1, relationship, strength)
            logger.info(f"Nodes {node_id1} and {node_id2} connected with '{relationship}' (strength: {strength}).")
        else:
            logger.warning("One or both node IDs not found.")

    def apply_network_stress(self, stress_pattern):
        """
        Applies a "stress pattern" to the network, affecting node energy
        and connection stress (simulating Cube's stress propagation).
        """
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            node.apply_external_stress(stress_pattern.get(node_id, 0.0))  # Node-specific stress
            for connected_node_id in node.get_connections():
                node.update_connection_stress(connected_node_id, stress_pattern.get(connected_node_id, 0.0))

    def simulate_network_dynamics(self, growth_laws, external_stress, time_steps=10):
        """
        Simulates the network's evolution over time, applying growth laws
        and external stress iteratively.
        """
        for t in range(time_steps):
            self.apply_network_stress(external_stress)
            growth_laws.apply(self.nodes.values(), external_stress)
            # ... other simulation logic (replication, etc.) ...

    def get_highest_priority_nodes(self, count=5):
        """Placeholder for priority-based node selection."""
        # Implement a priority mechanism if needed
        # For now, just return random nodes
        return random.sample(list(self.nodes.keys()), min(count, len(self.nodes)))

# core/supernode_core.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
supernode_core.py

Core implementation of the SuperNode quantum-inspired neural processing engine.
This module provides the fundamental building blocks for the Kaleidoscope AI system.
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import numpy as np
import threading
import uuid
import logging
import math
import random
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Any, Union, Callable
from enum import Enum, auto
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

# Configure logging
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s"
)
logger = logging.getLogger("SuperNodeCore")

# Verify and setup virtual environment
def setup_virtual_environment():
    """Set up virtual environment if not already activated"""
    venv_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), ".venv")
    
    # Check if we're already in a virtual environment
    if hasattr(sys, 'real_prefix') or (hasattr(sys, 'base_prefix') and sys.base_prefix != sys.prefix):
        logger.info("Virtual environment already activated")
        return True
    
    # Create virtual environment if not exists
    if not os.path.exists(venv_dir):
        try:
            subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "venv", venv_dir], check=True)
            logger.info(f"Created virtual environment at {venv_dir}")
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating virtual environment: {e}")
            return False
    
    # Activate virtual environment
    if sys.platform == 'win32':
        activate_script = os.path.join(venv_dir, "Scripts", "activate.bat")
        activate_cmd = f'"{activate_script}"'
    else:
        activate_script = os.path.join(venv_dir, "bin", "activate")
        activate_cmd = f'source "{activate_script}"'
    
    # Install dependencies
    try:
        subprocess.run(activate_cmd, shell=True, check=True)
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", "-r", "requirements.txt"], check=True)
        logger.info("Installed dependencies")
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        logger.error(f"Error activating/installing: {e}")
        return False
    
    return True

class ResonanceMode(Enum):
    """
    Different modes of resonance for data processing.
    """
    HARMONIC = auto()  # Purely sinusoidal (simplified)
    DAMPED = auto()    # Sinusoidal with damping
    HYBRID = auto()    # Combination of harmonic and damped

class AI_Core:
    """
    Main execution loop and system orchestrator for the Unified AI Core,
    now integrating a local GPT processor.
    """

    class Node:  # Placeholder for actual Node class

            # Create different types of nodes, ensuring TextNode gets the GPT processor

            node_id = f"NodeInit_{i}"

                node = TextNode(core_laws=core_laws, gpt_processor=self.gpt_processor, node_id=node_id)
            elif node_type_choice == "visual" and VisualNode:  # Check if VisualNode exists
                node = VisualNode(core_laws=core_laws, node_id=node_id)

                # Fallback or generic node type placeholder
                # You might need a GenericNode class or use BaseNode if applicable
                from .BaseNode import BaseNode  # Import BaseNode if needed for generic nodes
                node = BaseNode(node_type="generic", core_laws=core_laws, node_id=node_id)
                # Assign placeholder capabilities or state if BaseNode is used directly
                node.state = node.CoreState() if not hasattr(node, 'state') else node.state  # Ensure state exists

            self.node_manager.register_node(node)
        logger.info(f"Base nodes initialized.")

    def start(self, execution_cycles=10, interval=1):
        """Starts the main execution loop."""
        logger.info("Starting AI Core execution loop...")
        self.running = True
        for cycle in range(execution_cycles):
            if not self.running:
                logger.info("AI Core stopping.")
                break
            logger.info(f"--- Cycle {cycle + 1}/{execution_cycles} ---")
            start_time = time.time()

            # 1. Simulate receiving data (replace with real data source)
            input_data = self._get_simulated_input()

            # 2. Process data through perspectives
            perspective_results = self.perspective_manager.process_perspectives(input_data.get("text", ""))  # Example: text only

            # 3. Distribute tasks to nodes
            node_results = self._distribute_and_process(input_data)

            # 4. Apply Growth Laws (Energy update, Replication check)
            self.growth_laws.apply(self.node_manager.nodes.values())  # Pass node objects

            # 5. Replication based on Growth Laws decision
            self._handle_replication()

            # 6. Mirrored Network Sync
            self.mirrored_network.synchronize(self.node_manager.nodes)  # Needs node data

            # 7. Update Memory Graph
            self._update_memory_graph(node_results)

            # 8. Pattern Recognition
            combined_data = self._combine_results(node_results, perspective_results)
            recognized_patterns = self.pattern_recognizer.recognize_patterns(combined_data)

            # 9. Logging and Status Update
            cycle_duration = time.time() - start_time
            self._log_cycle_status(cycle, cycle_duration, recognized_patterns)

            time.sleep(interval)

        self.running = False
        self.memory_graph.save_memory()  # Final save
        logger.info("AI Core execution loop finished.")

    def stop(self):
        """Stops the execution loop."""
        logger.info("Received stop signal.")
        self.running = False

        """Generates sample input data for simulation."""

        text_input = random.choice([

        # image_data = np.random.rand(100, 100, 3) # Example image data

    def _distribute_and_process(self, input_data):
        """Distributes data to relevant nodes and collects results."""
        results = {}
        for node_id, node in self.node_manager.nodes.items():
            # Basic routing based on node type (can be more sophisticated)
            if isinstance(node, TextNode) and "text" in input_data:
                result = node.process_text(input_data["text"])
                results[node_id] = result
            elif isinstance(node, VisualNode) and "image" in input_data:
                result = node.process_image(input_data["image"])
                results[node_id] = result
            # Add processing for other node types if needed
        return results

    def _handle_replication(self):
        """Checks nodes for replication conditions and performs replication."""
        nodes_to_add = []
        nodes_to_update_energy = {}  # Store energy changes

        for node_id, node in list(self.node_manager.nodes.items()):  # Iterate over copy
            if hasattr(node, 'can_replicate') and node.can_replicate():  # Check if method exists
                new_node_dna = node.replicate_dna()  # Assuming DNA replication logic
                if new_node_dna:
                    # Use SeedManager to initialize the new node based on DNA
                    new_node_id = self.seed_manager.initialize_from_seed(
                        seed_id=f"seed_{node_id}_{time.time()}",  # Generate a unique seed ID
                        parent_dna=new_node_dna,
                        parent_id=node_id
                    )
                    # Create the actual node instance (assuming SeedManager returns ID/config)
                    # This part needs refinement based on how SeedManager provides node info
                    # For now, let's assume it returns an ID and we create a default node type
                    if new_node_id:
                        # Determine node type based on parent or DNA, simplified here
                        NewNodeClass = type(node)  # Replicate the same type
                        new_node_instance = NewNodeClass(
                            core_laws=node.core_laws.mutate(),  # Mutate laws
                            gpt_processor=self.gpt_processor if isinstance(node, TextNode) else None,  # Pass GPT if TextNode
                            node_id=new_node_id
                        )
                        nodes_to_add.append(new_node_instance)
                        # Manage energy transfer
                        transfer_energy = node.state.energy * 0.4  # Example energy split
                        nodes_to_update_energy[node_id] = -transfer_energy
                        nodes_to_update_energy[new_node_id] = transfer_energy  # Initial energy for child
                        logger.info(f"Node {node_id} replicated to {new_node_id}")

        # Add new nodes and update energy outside the loop to avoid modifying dict during iteration
        for new_node in nodes_to_add:
            self.node_manager.register_node(new_node)
            if new_node.id in nodes_to_update_energy:  # Assign initial energy
                new_node.state.energy = nodes_to_update_energy[new_node.id]

        for node_id, energy_change in nodes_to_update_energy.items():
            if node_id in self.node_manager.nodes and node_id not in [n.id for n in nodes_to_add]:  # Update parent energy
                self.node_manager.nodes[node_id].state.energy += energy_change

    def _update_memory_graph(self, node_results):
        """Updates the central memory graph with relationships."""
        # Example: Add relationship based on shared entities or high confidence
        for node_id, result in node_results.items():
            if result and result.get("status") == "success" and "entities" in result:
                for entity, label in result["entities"]:
                    # Add node for entity if not exists
                    if not self.memory_graph.graph.has_node(entity):
                        self.memory_graph.add_node(entity, type=label)
                    # Add relationship between processing node and entity
                    self.memory_graph.connect_nodes(node_id, entity, "processed")

    def _combine_results(self, node_results, perspective_results):
        """Combines results from nodes and perspectives for pattern recognition."""
        # Simple combination for now, can be more complex
        combined = {"nodes": node_results, "perspectives": perspective_results}
        # Flatten or structure data appropriately for PatternRecognition module
        # Example: extract all text summaries into one list
        all_summaries = []
        for res in node_results.values():
            if res and res.get("summary"):
                all_summaries.append(res["summary"])
        combined["flat_text"] = " ".join(all_summaries)
        return combined

    def _log_cycle_status(self, cycle, duration, patterns):
        """Logs the status of the system after each cycle."""
        num_nodes = self.node_manager.get_node_count()
        status = self.node_manager.get_all_statuses()
        avg_energy = np.mean([s.get('energy', 0) for s in status.values()]) if status else 0
        logger.info(f"Cycle {cycle + 1} completed in {duration:.4f}s.")
        logger.info(f"Nodes: {num_nodes}, Avg Energy: {avg_energy:.2f}")
        if patterns:
            logger.info(f"Patterns Recognized: {len(patterns)}")
        # Optionally log memory graph status
        # logger.info(f"Memory Graph: {self.memory_graph.get_status()}")


# Main execution block

class BaseNode:
    """

    and methods to simulate stress-based adaptation.
    """

        def __init__(self):
            self.energy = random.uniform(20, 50)

    def __init__(self, node_id=None, core_laws=None, node_type="base"):
        self.id = node_id if node_id else str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.core_laws = core_laws
        self.state = BaseNode.CoreState()
        self.node_type = node_type
        self.connections = {}  # {other_node_id: {"relationship": str, "strength": float, "stress": float}}

    def apply_external_stress(self, stress_signal):
        """
        Simulates how an external "stress" signal affects the node.
        This is a basic example; you can make it more complex.
        """
        self.state.energy -= stress_signal * 0.1  # Example: Stress reduces energy
        # You could also imagine stress affecting replication, mutation, etc.

        """
        Adds a connection with a "relationship" type and initial "strength".
        Also initializes "stress" to 0.

# core/GrowthLaws.py
import logging
import random
import math
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class GrowthLaws:
    """
    Simulates how "stress" and other factors influence node behavior,
    drawing inspiration from the Cube's dynamic string concept.
    """

    def __init__(self,
                 energy_threshold=100,
                 replication_chance=0.1,
                 base_stress=1.0,  # Base level of environmental stress
                 stress_sensitivity=0.1,  # How much nodes react to stress
                 energy_gain_range=(5, 15)):  # Range for random energy gain
        self.energy_threshold = energy_threshold
        self.replication_chance = replication_chance
        self.base_stress = base_stress
        self.stress_sensitivity = stress_sensitivity
        self.energy_gain_range = energy_gain_range

    def apply(self, nodes, external_stress):
        """
        Applies growth laws, now incorporating external stress.
        """
        for node in nodes:
            self._simulate_energy_dynamics(node, external_stress)
            self._check_replication(node, external_stress)
            # ... other logic ...

    def _simulate_energy_dynamics(self, node, external_stress):
        """
        Energy gain/decay influenced by external stress.
        """
        # "Stress" modulates energy gain (Cube principle)
        effective_stress = self.base_stress + external_stress * self.stress_sensitivity
        gain = random.uniform(self.energy_gain_range[0], self.energy_gain_range[1]) * effective_stress
        node.state.energy += gain

        # Energy decay (simplified)
        node.state.energy -= 1
        node.state.energy = max(0, node.state.energy)

    def _check_replication(self, node, external_stress):
        """
        Replication chance also modulated by stress.
        """
        adaptive_replication_chance = self.replication_chance * (node.state.energy / self.energy_threshold) * external_stress
        if random.random() < adaptive_replication_chance:
            node.state.ready_to_replicate = True
        else:
            node.state.ready_to_replicate = False

    def mutate(self):
        """Placeholder for mutation logic."""
        # Implement mutation logic here (e.g., changing node properties)
        pass

# core/NodeManager.py
import logging
import heapq
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class NodeManager:
    """
    Enhanced NodeManager that manages nodes and their connections,
    including methods to apply stress and simulate network-wide effects.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes: Dict[str, Any] = {}  # {node_id: node_object}
        self.priority_queue = []  # Placeholder for task prioritization
        self.memory_graph = None  # Placeholder for memory graph integration

    def register_node(self, node):
        """Registers a node with the manager."""
        if node.id not in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node.id] = node
            logger.info(f"Registered node: {node.id} ({node.node_type})")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Node ID already exists: {node.id}")

    def unregister_node(self, node_id: str):
        """Unregisters a node from the manager."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            del self.nodes[node_id]
            logger.info(f"Unregistered node: {node_id}")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Node ID not found: {node_id}")

    def get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Retrieves a node by its ID."""
        return self.nodes.get(node_id)

    def get_node_count(self) -> int:
        """Gets the total number of registered nodes."""
        return len(self.nodes)

    def get_all_statuses(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Gets the status of all registered nodes."""
        statuses = {}
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            if hasattr(node, 'state'):
                statuses[node_id] = {"energy": node.state.energy, "status": "active"}  # Example
            else:
                statuses[node_id] = {"status": "unknown"}
        return statuses

    def find_nodes_by_type(self, node_type: str) -> List[Any]:
        """Finds nodes of a specific type."""
        return [node for node in self.nodes.values() if node.node_type == node_type]

    def connect_nodes(self, node_id1, node_id2, relationship, strength=1.0):
        """
        Connects two nodes, creating a "string" relationship.
        """
        if node_id1 in self.nodes and node_id2 in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node_id1].add_connection(node_id2, relationship, strength)
            self.nodes[node_id2].add_connection(node_id1, relationship, strength)
            logger.info(f"Nodes {node_id1} and {node_id2} connected with '{relationship}' (strength: {strength}).")
        else:
            logger.warning("One or both node IDs not found.")

    def apply_network_stress(self, stress_pattern):
        """
        Applies a "stress pattern" to the network, affecting node energy
        and connection stress (simulating Cube's stress propagation).
        """
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            node.apply_external_stress(stress_pattern.get(node_id, 0.0))  # Node-specific stress
            for connected_node_id in node.get_connections():
                node.update_connection_stress(connected_node_id, stress_pattern.get(connected_node_id, 0.0))

    def simulate_network_dynamics(self, growth_laws, external_stress, time_steps=10):
        """
        Simulates the network's evolution over time, applying growth laws
        and external stress iteratively.
        """
        for t in range(time_steps):
            self.apply_network_stress(external_stress)
            growth_laws.apply(self.nodes.values(), external_stress)
            # ... other simulation logic (replication, etc.) ...

    def get_highest_priority_nodes(self, count=5):
        """Placeholder for priority-based node selection."""
        # Implement a priority mechanism if needed
        # For now, just return random nodes
        return random.sample(list(self.nodes.keys()), min(count, len(self.nodes)))

# core/supernode_core.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
supernode_core.py

Core implementation of the SuperNode quantum-inspired neural processing engine.
This module provides the fundamental building blocks for the Kaleidoscope AI system.
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import numpy as np
import threading
import uuid
import logging
import math
import random
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Any, Union, Callable
from enum import Enum, auto
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

# Configure logging
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s"
)
logger = logging.getLogger("SuperNodeCore")

# Verify and setup virtual environment
def setup_virtual_environment():
    """Set up virtual environment if not already activated"""
    venv_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), ".venv")
    
    # Check if we're already in a virtual environment
    if hasattr(sys, 'real_prefix') or (hasattr(sys, 'base_prefix') and sys.base_prefix != sys.prefix):
        logger.info("Virtual environment already activated")
        return True
    
    # Create virtual environment if not exists
    if not os.path.exists(venv_dir):
        try:
            subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "venv", venv_dir], check=True)
            logger.info(f"Created virtual environment at {venv_dir}")
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating virtual environment: {e}")
            return False
    
    # Activate virtual environment
    if sys.platform == 'win32':
        activate_script = os.path.join(venv_dir, "Scripts", "activate.bat")
        activate_cmd = f'"{activate_script}"'
    else:
        activate_script = os.path.join(venv_dir, "bin", "activate")
        activate_cmd = f'source "{activate_script}"'
    
    # Install dependencies
    try:
        subprocess.run(activate_cmd, shell=True, check=True)
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", "-r", "requirements.txt"], check=True)
        logger.info("Installed dependencies")
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        logger.error(f"Error activating/installing: {e}")
        return False
    
    return True

    HYBRID = auto()    # Combination of harmonic and damped

        return cls(**data)

        return cls(**data)

    def start(self, execution_cycles=10, interval=1):
        """Starts the main execution loop."""
        logger.info("Starting AI Core execution loop...")
        self.running = True
        for cycle in range(execution_cycles):
            if not self.running:
                logger.info("AI Core stopping.")
                break
            logger.info(f"--- Cycle {cycle + 1}/{execution_cycles} ---")

            # 9. Logging and Status Update
            cycle_duration = time.time() - start_time
            self._log_cycle_status(cycle, cycle_duration, recognized_patterns)

            time.sleep(interval)

        self.running = False
        self.memory_graph.save_memory()  # Final save
        logger.info("AI Core execution loop finished.")

        """Stops the execution loop."""

    def _distribute_and_process(self, input_data):
        """Distributes data to relevant nodes and collects results."""
        results = {}
        for node_id, node in self.node_manager.nodes.items():
            # Basic routing based on node type (can be more sophisticated)

                results[node_id] = result
            elif isinstance(node, VisualNode) and "image" in input_data:
                result = node.process_image(input_data["image"])
                results[node_id] = result
            # Add processing for other node types if needed
        return results

    def _handle_replication(self):
        """Checks nodes for replication conditions and performs replication."""
        nodes_to_add = []
        nodes_to_update_energy = {}  # Store energy changes

        for node_id, node in list(self.node_manager.nodes.items()):  # Iterate over copy

                new_node_dna = node.replicate_dna()  # Assuming DNA replication logic

                        seed_id=f"seed_{node_id}_{time.time()}",  # Generate a unique seed ID

                        parent_id=node_id
                    )
                    # Create the actual node instance (assuming SeedManager returns ID/config)

                        # Determine node type based on parent or DNA, simplified here

                            core_laws=node.core_laws.mutate(),  # Mutate laws
                            gpt_processor=self.gpt_processor if isinstance(node, TextNode) else None,  # Pass GPT if TextNode
                            node_id=new_node_id
                        )
                        nodes_to_add.append(new_node_instance)
                        # Manage energy transfer
                        transfer_energy = node.state.energy * 0.4  # Example energy split
                        nodes_to_update_energy[node_id] = -transfer_energy
                        nodes_to_update_energy[new_node_id] = transfer_energy  # Initial energy for child
                        logger.info(f"Node {node_id} replicated to {new_node_id}")

        # Add new nodes and update energy outside the loop to avoid modifying dict during iteration
        for new_node in nodes_to_add:
            self.node_manager.register_node(new_node)
            if new_node.id in nodes_to_update_energy:  # Assign initial energy
                new_node.state.energy = nodes_to_update_energy[new_node.id]

        for node_id, energy_change in nodes_to_update_energy.items():
            if node_id in self.node_manager.nodes and node_id not in [n.id for n in nodes_to_add]:  # Update parent energy
                self.node_manager.nodes[node_id].state.energy += energy_change

    def _update_memory_graph(self, node_results):
        """Updates the central memory graph with relationships."""
        # Example: Add relationship based on shared entities or high confidence
        for node_id, result in node_results.items():
            if result and result.get("status") == "success" and "entities" in result:
                for entity, label in result["entities"]:

                    if not self.memory_graph.graph.has_node(entity):
                        self.memory_graph.add_node(entity, type=label)
                    # Add relationship between processing node and entity
                    self.memory_graph.connect_nodes(node_id, entity, "processed")

    def _combine_results(self, node_results, perspective_results):
        """Combines results from nodes and perspectives for pattern recognition."""
        # Simple combination for now, can be more complex
        combined = {"nodes": node_results, "perspectives": perspective_results}

                all_summaries.append(res["summary"])
        combined["flat_text"] = " ".join(all_summaries)
        return combined

    def _log_cycle_status(self, cycle, duration, patterns):
        """Logs the status of the system after each cycle."""
        num_nodes = self.node_manager.get_node_count()
        status = self.node_manager.get_all_statuses()

        if patterns:
            logger.info(f"Patterns Recognized: {len(patterns)}")
        # Optionally log memory graph status
        # logger.info(f"Memory Graph: {self.memory_graph.get_status()}")


# Main execution block

class BaseNode:
    """
    Enhanced BaseNode with "string" properties to connect to other nodes,
    and methods to simulate stress-based adaptation.
    """

    class CoreState:
        def __init__(self):
            self.energy = random.uniform(20, 50)
            self.ready_to_replicate = False
            self.dna = json.dumps({"genes": ["A", "C", "G", "T"], "traits": {"size": 1.0, "speed": 1.0}})
            self.metadata = {"created_at": str(uuid.uuid4()), "memory": []}

    def __init__(self, node_id=None, core_laws=None, node_type="base"):
        self.id = node_id if node_id else str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.core_laws = core_laws
        self.state = BaseNode.CoreState()
        self.node_type = node_type
        self.connections = {}  # {other_node_id: {"relationship": str, "strength": float, "stress": float}}

    def apply_external_stress(self, stress_signal):
        """
        Simulates how an external "stress" signal affects the node.
        This is a basic example; you can make it more complex.
        """
        self.state.energy -= stress_signal * 0.1  # Example: Stress reduces energy
        # You could also imagine stress affecting replication, mutation, etc.

    def add_connection(self, other_node_id, relationship, strength=1.0):
        """
        Adds a connection with a "relationship" type and initial "strength".
        Also initializes "stress" to 0.
        """
        self.connections[other_node_id] = {"relationship": relationship, "strength": strength, "stress": 0.0}

    def update_connection_stress(self, other_node_id, stress_delta):
        """
        Updates the "stress" level on a connection, simulating how external
        factors change the relationship between nodes (Cube principle).
        """
        if other_node_id in self.connections:
            self.connections[other_node_id]["stress"] += stress_delta
            # You might also update the connection "strength" based on stress here

        """Placeholder for data processing logic."""
        # ... data processing logic ...

# core/GrowthLaws.py
import logging
import random
import math
import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class GrowthLaws:
    """
    Simulates how "stress" and other factors influence node behavior,
    drawing inspiration from the Cube's dynamic string concept.
    """

    def __init__(self,
                 energy_threshold=100,
                 replication_chance=0.1,
                 base_stress=1.0,  # Base level of environmental stress
                 stress_sensitivity=0.1,  # How much nodes react to stress
                 energy_gain_range=(5, 15)):  # Range for random energy gain
        self.energy_threshold = energy_threshold
        self.replication_chance = replication_chance
        self.base_stress = base_stress
        self.stress_sensitivity = stress_sensitivity
        self.energy_gain_range = energy_gain_range

    def apply(self, nodes, external_stress):
        """
        Applies growth laws, now incorporating external stress.
        """
        for node in nodes:
            self._simulate_energy_dynamics(node, external_stress)
            self._check_replication(node, external_stress)
            # ... other logic ...

    def _simulate_energy_dynamics(self, node, external_stress):
        """
        Energy gain/decay influenced by external stress.
        """
        # "Stress" modulates energy gain (Cube principle)
        effective_stress = self.base_stress + external_stress * self.stress_sensitivity
        gain = random.uniform(self.energy_gain_range[0], self.energy_gain_range[1]) * effective_stress
        node.state.energy += gain

        # Energy decay (simplified)
        node.state.energy -= 1
        node.state.energy = max(0, node.state.energy)

    def _check_replication(self, node, external_stress):
        """
        Replication chance also modulated by stress.
        """
        adaptive_replication_chance = self.replication_chance * (node.state.energy / self.energy_threshold) * external_stress
        if random.random() < adaptive_replication_chance:
            node.state.ready_to_replicate = True
        else:
            node.state.ready_to_replicate = False

    def mutate(self):
        """Placeholder for mutation logic."""
        # Implement mutation logic here (e.g., changing node properties)
        pass

# core/NodeManager.py
import logging
import heapq
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class NodeManager:
    """
    Enhanced NodeManager that manages nodes and their connections,
    including methods to apply stress and simulate network-wide effects.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes: Dict[str, Any] = {}  # {node_id: node_object}
        self.priority_queue = []  # Placeholder for task prioritization
        self.memory_graph = None  # Placeholder for memory graph integration

    def register_node(self, node):
        """Registers a node with the manager."""
        if node.id not in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node.id] = node
            logger.info(f"Registered node: {node.id} ({node.node_type})")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Node ID already exists: {node.id}")

    def unregister_node(self, node_id: str):
        """Unregisters a node from the manager."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            del self.nodes[node_id]
            logger.info(f"Unregistered node: {node_id}")
        else:
            logger.warning(f"Node ID not found: {node_id}")

    def get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Retrieves a node by its ID."""
        return self.nodes.get(node_id)

    def get_node_count(self) -> int:
        """Gets the total number of registered nodes."""
        return len(self.nodes)

    def get_all_statuses(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Gets the status of all registered nodes."""
        statuses = {}
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            if hasattr(node, 'state'):
                statuses[node_id] = {"energy": node.state.energy, "status": "active"}  # Example
            else:
                statuses[node_id] = {"status": "unknown"}
        return statuses

    def find_nodes_by_type(self, node_type: str) -> List[Any]:
        """Finds nodes of a specific type."""
        return [node for node in self.nodes.values() if node.node_type == node_type]

    def connect_nodes(self, node_id1, node_id2, relationship, strength=1.0):
        """
        Connects two nodes, creating a "string" relationship.
        """
        if node_id1 in self.nodes and node_id2 in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node_id1].add_connection(node_id2, relationship, strength)
            self.nodes[node_id2].add_connection(node_id1, relationship, strength)
            logger.info(f"Nodes {node_id1} and {node_id2} connected with '{relationship}' (strength: {strength}).")
        else:
            logger.warning("One or both node IDs not found.")

    def apply_network_stress(self, stress_pattern):
        """
        Applies a "stress pattern" to the network, affecting node energy
        and connection stress (simulating Cube's stress propagation).
        """
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            node.apply_external_stress(stress_pattern.get(node_id, 0.0))  # Node-specific stress
            for connected_node_id in node.get_connections():
                node.update_connection_stress(connected_node_id, stress_pattern.get(connected_node_id, 0.0))

    def simulate_network_dynamics(self, growth_laws, external_stress, time_steps=10):
        """
        Simulates the network's evolution over time, applying growth laws
        and external stress iteratively.
        """
        for t in range(time_steps):
            self.apply_network_stress(external_stress)
            growth_laws.apply(self.nodes.values(), external_stress)
            # ... other simulation logic (replication, etc.) ...

    def get_highest_priority_nodes(self, count=5):
        """Placeholder for priority-based node selection."""
        # Implement a priority mechanism if needed
        # For now, just return random nodes
        return random.sample(list(self.nodes.keys()), min(count, len(self.nodes)))

    """Set up virtual environment if not already activated"""

        return True
